# Third-party notices

## Spotifly playback bridge and authentication support

Portions of `Rune/src/playback`, `Sources/Spotty/Spotify`, and `Sources/SpottyGateway` are adapted from
[Spotifly](https://github.com/ralph/Spotifly), commit
`35991ac25a04aa14f8839d88f46129da6c6b59c0`. The Connect command and metadata request shapes are
adapted from commit `bcb522675e9657599faa007c531c2159e506246f`.

## librespot

The Rust app links upstream [librespot](https://github.com/librespot-org/librespot), pinned to
commit `939dc5ee9d833e1980f9495241219d9d4868a061`, with local patches to `librespot-connect`,
`librespot-core`, and `librespot-playback` described in
`Backend/spotty-playback/vendor/librespot/README.md`. librespot is MIT licensed and carries the
copyright below.

## Rust dependency graph

Apple Silicon packaging generates a dependency inventory from `Rune/Cargo.lock` with
`Scripts/generate-rune-notices.py`. The app bundle includes the inventory and its
license texts in `Contents/Resources`.

## Generated Rune dependency inventory

- Target: `aarch64-apple-darwin`
- Cargo graph edges: `normal,build`
- Root package: `spotty-rune v0.1.0`
- Third-party packages: 393
- Unique full license texts: 272
- Machine-readable inventory: [ThirdPartyNotices.json](ThirdPartyNotices.json)

The licenses directory contains the collected license and notice texts. The inventory records each file's digest, source location, and package association. It labels canonical license references and package declarations where the upstream archive omits the license text.

Scripts/generate-rune-notices.py regenerates the inventory from the locked graph and fails when a package has neither a discoverable license text nor an explicit override.
